00587:         )

00597:         )

00601:                 slot
00602:             ]
00603:         )
00604:

00607:                 f"slot {slot} tally mismatch"
00608:             )

00612:             1,

00614:             True,

------------------------------------------------------------------------------

00837:                         ],

00842:                         "--json",
00843:                     ],
00844:                     check=False,
00845:                 )
00846: 
00847:                 end_ns = (
00848:                     time.perf_counter_ns()
00849:                 )
00850: 
00851:                 latency_ms = (
00852:                     end_ns
00853:                     - start_ns
00854:                 ) / 1_000_000.0
00855:

00860:                         + ".json"

00873:                 ).write_text(
00874:                     cp.stderr or "",
00875:                     encoding="utf-8",
00876:                     newline="\n",
00877:                 )
00878: 
00879:                 if cp.returncode != 0:
00880:                     raise RuntimeError(
00881:                         "cast send failed: "
00882:                         + (
00883:                             cp.stderr
00884:                             or ""
00885:                         ).strip()
00886:                     )
00887: 
00888:                 (
00889:                     ok,
00890:                     tx_hash,
00891:                     gas_used,
00892:                     _,
00893:                 ) = v2.receipt_info(
00894:                     cp.stdout
00895:                 )
00896: 
00897:                 if not ok:
00898:                     raise RuntimeError(
00899:                         "receipt status failure"
00900:                     )
00901: 
00902:                 if not tx_hash:
00903:                     raise RuntimeError(
00904:                         "empty transaction hash"
00905:                     )
00906: 
00907:                 if gas_used is None:
00908:                     raise RuntimeError(
00909:                         "missing gasUsed"
00910:                     )
00911: 
00912:                 correctness = (
00913:                     verify_single_vote_state(
00914:                         v2,
00915:                         cast,

00917:                         direct,

00919:                         expected_tally,
00920:                     )
00921:                 )
00922: 
00923:                 row = {
00924:                     "kind":
00925:                         kind,
00926:                     "iteration":
00927:                         iteration,
00928:                     "repetition_id":
00929:                         repetition_id,
00930:                     "ballot_id":
00931:                         EXPECTED_BALLOT_ID,
00932:                     "candidate_index":
00933:                         EXPECTED_CANDIDATE_INDEX,

00935:                         tx_hash,

00949:

00951:

00954:                         "PASS",

00026: 
00027: FINAL SAMPLE PLAN
00028: -----------------
00029: Warm-up iterations: 5.
00030: Measured iterations: 20.
00031: Total final iterations: 25.
00032: 
00033: Every iteration begins from a completely fresh Anvil chain.
00034: 
00035: Every iteration uses the exact same frozen ballot-001 proof/ciphertext fixture.
00036: 
00037: Warm-ups execute first and are preserved but excluded from final statistics.
00038: 
00039: TIMING METRIC
00040: -------------
00041: Start:
00042: immediately before invoking cast send for castVote.
00043: 
00044: Stop:
00045: immediately after the cast send process returns with the receipt output.
00046: 
00047: Metric:
00048: local cast-send-start to receipt-return wall-clock latency.
00049: 
00050: Outside timed interval:
00051: - Anvil startup,
00052: - verifier deployment,
00053: - DIRECT deployment,
00054: - configure,
00055: - activate,
00056: - fixture loading,
00057: - initial-state checks,
00058: - receipt parsing,
00059: - post-receipt state verification,
00060: - Anvil shutdown.
00061: 
00062: This metric is not:
00063: - Groth16 proving time,
00064: - witness-generation time,
00065: - public-network confirmation latency,
00066: - biometric/end-to-end voting latency.
00067: 
00068: PER-ITERATION RAW FIELDS
00069: ------------------------
00070: kind
00071: iteration
00072: repetition_id
00073: ballot_id
00074: candidate_index
00075: tx_hash
00076: receipt_status
00077: gas_used
00078: calldata_bytes
00079: latency_ms
00080: 
00081: CORRECTNESS GATES
00082: -----------------
00083: successful cast receipt;
00084: acceptedBallotCount == 1;
00085: usedNullifier(ballot-001 nullifier) == true;
00086: phase remains Active;
00087: all 16 encrypted tally slots equal the ballot-001 ciphertext contribution.
00088: 
00089: PARSER
00090: ------
00091: The new runner imports and reuses the frozen Runner V2 receipt parser.
00092: It does not restore the obsolete json.loads(full_stdout) assumption.
00093: 
00094: PILOT POLICY
00095: ------------
00096: Exactly one non-final E2E pilot is available through --pilot-one.
00097: 
00098: The pilot is:
00099: PILOT_ONLY_NOT_FINAL_BENCHMARK.
00100: 
00101: Pilot observations must never be included in final statistics.
00102: 
00103: FINAL EXECUTION POLICY
00104: ----------------------
00105: The final 25-iteration campaign must start from iteration 1.
00106: 
00107: On any final execution failure:
00108: - preserve all partial files,
00109: - do not delete observations,
00110: - do not silently restart,
00111: - do not reconstruct latency,
00112: - diagnose before any new attempt.
00113: 
00114: STATISTICAL POLICY
00115: ------------------
00116: Only the 20 measured iterations enter final microbenchmark statistics.
00117: 
00118: All 5 warm-ups remain preserved.
00119: 
00120: No silent outlier deletion.
00121: 
00122: Report:
00123: mean,
00124: median,
00125: min,
00126: max,
00127: sample standard deviation,
00128: nearest-rank p95.
00129: 
00130: R1 EXECUTION STATUS
00131: -------------------
00132: FINAL_MICROBENCHMARK_STARTED=NO
00133: ANVIL_STARTED=NO
00134: TRANSACTION_SUBMITTED=NO

00170: def receipt_info(text):
00171:     decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
00172: 
00173:     stripped = text.lstrip()
00174: 
00175:     if not stripped:
00176:         raise RuntimeError(
00177:             "empty cast receipt stdout"
00178:         )
00179: 
00180:     try:
00181:         obj, end = decoder.raw_decode(
00182:             stripped
00183:         )
00184:     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
00185:         raise RuntimeError(
00186:             "could not decode leading "
00187:             "cast receipt JSON"
00188:         ) from exc
00189: 
00190:     if not isinstance(
00191:         obj,
00192:         dict,
00193:     ):
00194:         raise RuntimeError(
00195:             "leading cast receipt JSON "
00196:             "is not an object"
00197:         )
00198: 
00199:     trailing = stripped[end:]
00200: 
00201:     # Full raw stdout is preserved separately in the receipt
00202:     # artifact before this parser is called.  A sporadic Foundry/
00203:     # Alloy diagnostic may therefore follow a valid receipt JSON.
00204:     #
00205:     # Do not interpret a second JSON object as harmless log text.
00206:     trailing_nonws = trailing.lstrip()
00207: 
00208:     if trailing_nonws.startswith(
00209:         ("{", "[")
00210:     ):
00211:         try:
00212:             decoder.raw_decode(
00213:                 trailing_nonws
00214:             )
00215:         except json.JSONDecodeError:
00216:             pass
00217:         else:
00218:             raise RuntimeError(
00219:                 "multiple top-level JSON values "
00220:                 "in cast receipt stdout"
00221:             )
00222: 
00223:     status = obj.get(
00224:         "status"
00225:     )
00226: 
00227:     if isinstance(
00228:         status,
00229:         str,
00230:     ):
00231:         ok = (
00232:             status.lower()
00233:             in {
00234:                 "0x1",
00235:                 "1",
00236:                 "success",
00237:             }
00238:         )
00239:     else:
00240:         ok = status == 1
00241: 
00242:     tx = (
00243:         obj.get(
00244:             "transactionHash"
00245:         )
00246:         or obj.get(
00247:             "transaction_hash"
00248:         )
00249:         or ""
00250:     )
00251: 
00252:     gas = (
00253:         obj.get(
00254:             "gasUsed"
00255:         )
00256:         or obj.get(
00257:             "gas_used"
00258:         )
00259:     )
00260: 
00261:     if gas is None:
00262:         gas_int = None
00263:     elif isinstance(
00264:         gas,
00265:         int,
00266:     ):
00267:         gas_int = gas
00268:     else:
00269:         gas_int = int(
00270:             str(gas),
00271:             0,
00272:         )
00273: 
00274:     return (
00275:         ok,
00276:         tx,
00277:         gas_int,
00278:         obj,
00279:     )

00683: def start_anvil(
00684:     anvil,
00685:     cast,
00686:     port,
00687:     log_file,
00688: ):
00689:     rpc = (
00690:         f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
00691:     )
00692: 
00693:     pre = run(
00694:         [
00695:             cast,
00696:             "block-number",
00697:             "--rpc-url",
00698:             rpc,
00699:         ],
00700:         check=False,
00701:     )
00702: 
00703:     if pre.returncode == 0:
00704:         raise RuntimeError(
00705:             f"RPC port {port} already in use"
00706:         )
00707: 
00708:     log_handle = open(
00709:         log_file,
00710:         "w",
00711:         encoding="utf-8",
00712:         newline="\n",
00713:     )
00714: 
00715:     process = subprocess.Popen(
00716:         [
00717:             anvil,
00718:             "--silent",
00719:             "--port",
00720:             str(port),
00721:             "--chain-id",
00722:             "31337",
00723:         ],
00724:         stdout=log_handle,
00725:         stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
00726:         text=True,
00727:     )
00728: 
00729:     for _ in range(100):
00730:         cp = run(
00731:             [
00732:                 cast,
00733:                 "block-number",
00734:                 "--rpc-url",
00735:                 rpc,
00736:             ],
00737:             check=False,
00738:         )
00739: 
00740:         if cp.returncode == 0:
00741:             return (
00742:                 process,
00743:                 log_handle,
00744:                 rpc,
00745:             )
00746: 
00747:         if process.poll() is not None:
00748:             break
00749: 
00750:         time.sleep(0.1)
00751: 
00752:     try:
00753:         stop_anvil(
00754:             process,
00755:             cast,
00756:             rpc,
00757:         )
00758:     finally:
00759:         log_handle.close()
00760: 
00761:     raise RuntimeError(
00762:         "Anvil failed to start"
00763:     )

00005: 0167:     return ""

00007: 0169: 
00008: 0170: def receipt_info(text):
00009: 0171:     decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
00010: 0172: 
00011: 0173:     stripped = text.lstrip()
00012: 0174: 
00013: 0175:     if not stripped:
00014: 0176:         raise RuntimeError(
00015: 0177:             "empty cast receipt stdout"
00016: 0178:         )
00017: 0179: 
00018: 0180:     try:
00019: 0181:         obj, end = decoder.raw_decode(
00020: 0182:             stripped
00021: 0183:         )
00022: 0184:     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
00023: 0185:         raise RuntimeError(
00024: 0186:             "could not decode leading "
00025: 0187:             "cast receipt JSON"
00026: 0188:         ) from exc
00027: 0189: 
00028: 0190:     if not isinstance(
00029: 0191:         obj,
00030: 0192:         dict,
00031: 0193:     ):
00032: 0194:         raise RuntimeError(
00033: 0195:             "leading cast receipt JSON "
00034: 0196:             "is not an object"
00035: 0197:         )
00036: 0198: 
00037: 0199:     trailing = stripped[end:]
00038: 0200: 
00039: 0201:     # Full raw stdout is preserved separately in the receipt
00040: 0202:     # artifact before this parser is called.  A sporadic Foundry/
00041: 0203:     # Alloy diagnostic may therefore follow a valid receipt JSON.
00042: 0204:     #
00043: 0205:     # Do not interpret a second JSON object as harmless log text.
00044: 0206:     trailing_nonws = trailing.lstrip()
00045: 0207: 
00046: 0208:     if trailing_nonws.startswith(
00047: 0209:         ("{", "[")
00048: 0210:     ):
00049: 0211:         try:
00050: 0212:             decoder.raw_decode(
00051: 0213:                 trailing_nonws
00052: 0214:             )
00053: 0215:         except json.JSONDecodeError:
00054: 0216:             pass
00055: 0217:         else:
00056: 0218:             raise RuntimeError(
00057: 0219:                 "multiple top-level JSON values "
00058: 0220:                 "in cast receipt stdout"
00059: 0221:             )
00060: 0222: 
00061: 0223:     status = obj.get(
00062: 0224:         "status"
00063: 0225:     )
00064: 0226: 
00065: 0227:     if isinstance(
00066: 0228:         status,
00067: 0229:         str,
00068: 0230:     ):
00069: 0231:         ok = (
00070: 0232:             status.lower()
00071: 0233:             in {
00072: 0234:                 "0x1",
00073: 0235:                 "1",
00074: 0236:                 "success",
00075: 0237:             }
00076: 0238:         )
00077: 0239:     else:
00078: 0240:         ok = status == 1
00079: 0241: 
00080: 0242:     tx = (
00081: 0243:         obj.get(
00082: 0244:             "transactionHash"
00083: 0245:         )
00084: 0246:         or obj.get(
00085: 0247:             "transaction_hash"
00086: 0248:         )
00087: 0249:         or ""
00088: 0250:     )
00089:

00091: REGION=stop_anvil,start_anvil,deploy_contracts LINES=597-802
00092: ========================================================================
00093: 0597:     )

00005: 0170: def receipt_info(text):
00006: 0171:     decoder = json.JSONDecoder()
00007: 0172: 
00008: 0173:     stripped = text.lstrip()
00009: 0174: 
00010: 0175:     if not stripped:
00011: 0176:         raise RuntimeError(
00012: 0177:             "empty cast receipt stdout"
00013: 0178:         )
00014: 0179: 
00015: 0180:     try:
00016: 0181:         obj, end = decoder.raw_decode(
00017: 0182:             stripped
00018: 0183:         )
00019: 0184:     except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
00020: 0185:         raise RuntimeError(
00021: 0186:             "could not decode leading "
00022: 0187:             "cast receipt JSON"
00023: 0188:         ) from exc
00024: 0189: 
00025: 0190:     if not isinstance(
00026: 0191:         obj,
00027: 0192:         dict,
00028: 0193:     ):
00029: 0194:         raise RuntimeError(
00030: 0195:             "leading cast receipt JSON "
00031: 0196:             "is not an object"
00032: 0197:         )
00033: 0198: 
00034: 0199:     trailing = stripped[end:]
00035: 0200: 
00036: 0201:     # Full raw stdout is preserved separately in the receipt
00037: 0202:     # artifact before this parser is called.  A sporadic Foundry/
00038: 0203:     # Alloy diagnostic may therefore follow a valid receipt JSON.
00039: 0204:     #
00040: 0205:     # Do not interpret a second JSON object as harmless log text.
00041: 0206:     trailing_nonws = trailing.lstrip()
00042: 0207: 
00043: 0208:     if trailing_nonws.startswith(
00044: 0209:         ("{", "[")
00045: 0210:     ):
00046: 0211:         try:
00047: 0212:             decoder.raw_decode(
00048: 0213:                 trailing_nonws
00049: 0214:             )
00050: 0215:         except json.JSONDecodeError:
00051: 0216:             pass
00052: 0217:         else:
00053: 0218:             raise RuntimeError(
00054: 0219:                 "multiple top-level JSON values "
00055: 0220:                 "in cast receipt stdout"
00056: 0221:             )
00057: 0222: 
00058: 0223:     status = obj.get(
00059: 0224:         "status"
00060: 0225:     )
00061: 0226: 
00062: 0227:     if isinstance(
00063: 0228:         status,
00064: 0229:         str,
00065: 0230:     ):
00066: 0231:         ok = (
00067: 0232:             status.lower()
00068: 0233:             in {
00069: 0234:                 "0x1",
00070: 0235:                 "1",
00071: 0236:                 "success",
00072: 0237:             }
00073: 0238:         )
00074: 0239:     else:
00075: 0240:         ok = status == 1
00076: 0241: 
00077: 0242:     tx = (
00078: 0243:         obj.get(
00079: 0244:             "transactionHash"
00080: 0245:         )
00081: 0246:         or obj.get(
00082: 0247:             "transaction_hash"
00083: 0248:         )
00084: 0249:         or ""
00085: 0250:     )
00086: 0251: 
00087: 0252:     gas = (
00088: 0253:         obj.get(
00089: 0254:             "gasUsed"
00090: 0255:         )
00091: 0256:         or obj.get(
00092: 0257:             "gas_used"
00093: 0258:         )
00094: 0259:     )
00095: 0260: 
00096: 0261:     if gas is None:
00097: 0262:         gas_int = None
00098: 0263:     elif isinstance(
00099: 0264:         gas,
00100: 0265:         int,
00101: 0266:     ):
00102: 0267:         gas_int = gas
00103: 0268:     else:
00104: 0269:         gas_int = int(
00105: 0270:             str(gas),
00106: 0271:             0,
00107: 0272:         )
00108: 0273: 
00109: 0274:     return (
00110: 0275:         ok,
00111: 0276:         tx,
00112: 0277:         gas_int,
00113: 0278:         obj,
00114: 0279:     )
